WWA (1997–2019) Card Archive

Rage in the Cage VI - February 2, 2003

2 February 2003
Content noticeA period document, served as recovered; withheld details are marked. It carries the humor, language, and content typical of its era and community — some of it crude, dated, or offensive, including material that wouldn't be written today. See Curation.
What this is The complete written script of the World Wrestling Alliance pay-per-view Rage in the Cage VI from Calgary, with commentary, entrances, eight cage-based matches including the Iron Man World Title main event, and a closing recap.
We open to a flyaround of the SaddleDome. The cold winter is obvious, and the barren trees in the parking lot look decidedly ill. As we flyaround, we xoom in to a black limouseune pulling into the parking lot. As we get closer and closer, we see the car get nearer and nearer. Finally, just as we have a good close shot, the limo stops, and the door opens. WWA President Matthew Warr emerges in a sharp black tuxedo. He gets out and straightens his collar. He talks with the chauffer... Warr: This time 2 months ago, the WWA was striving for survival. With no TV deal. Now, we are back, and tonight will be our crowning achievement. He pauses and stares at the camera Warr: Welcome ... To Rage In The Cage! The scene changes to see the WWA logo. It slowly fades into a picture of the late crew of STS-107. Gary Trudeau: Yesterday seven men and women lost their lives in the pursuit of a dream. A dream to explore the stars. They fulfilled the dream, but they paid the ultimate price. We in the World Wrestling Alliance mourn their loss along with the rest of the world. But the crew of the last flight of the Columbia would not want us to let this horrible accident end the dream. They would want us to take a moment to reflect and than to once more push forward. We have always looked to the stars and now they are within our reach. We cannot let the dream die. Their lives are at an end, but their legacy and the dream will live forever. The scene goes to a single picture of the Earth from space and than fades to black. The scene opens to an empty arena, with a steel cage erected on top of it. Al Pacino's voice begins to speak, recorded from the movie "Any Given Sunday". We are in hell now, And we can stay here, And get the shit kicked out of us, Images of Wes Sanders, Tracer Bullet, and Denrol, all after taking tremodous beatings. Or we can fight our way back into the light. We can climb out of hell. One inch at a time. A scene of Chance struggling to rise up, using the ropes to help him. You find out that life is a game of inches. Scenes of various high flyers going through the air and missing their moves. All in slow motion. Because in life the margin for error is so small. On half a step too late or too early. You don't quite make it. One half second too slow or too fast. And you don't quite catch it. The inches we need are everywhere around us. They are in every break of the game. Every minute, every second. The scene become faster, now it's various wrestlers, including Havoc, American Luchadores, and Nick Adams. They are all performing big moves. We fight for that inch. We tear ourselves and everyone around us, To pieces for that inch. We CLAW with our fingernails for that small inch. Scenes of Enigma, Jimmi Zion, Shaman, and Christyan Payne, delivering punches to opponents. Cause we know, When we add up all those inches, That's going to make the fucking difference, Between WINNING and LOSING, Between LIVING and DYING. Scenes of Jackson Kraven, Grunge, and Shane Dorian performing finishers to opponents. In any fight it is the guy who is willing to die, Who is going to win that inch. And I know if I'm going to have any life anymore, It is because I am still willing to fight and die for that inch. Scenes of Kash and Damian Thorne battling each other. Because that what LIVING is. The six inches in front of your face. A final scene of Ian Tense hugging the WWA World Title after winning it in the Birthday Brawl. The scene fades to black. World Wrestling Alliance Presents..... RITC VI LIVE! From The SaddleDome Calgary, Alberta, Canada Sunday, February 2nd 2003 BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOOOOOOOOOOOMMMMMMMMMMM! The thunderous pyrotechnics erupt from the ring posts as thwo phenomenal jets of water shoot up in the air, and as the fall down, they reveal some massive hanging icicles above the stageway. We xooma round the arena to see the fans going wild. We see the front row, where WWA WRookies Damian Ryan, hein Bloeck and Ender are all sat, seperately of course. We finally zoom in to see the dream team of commentary, Jimmy Rose and "Fat" Tony Valentino. They are barely audible Jimmy: LADIES AND GENTLEMEN! IT IS TIME! FOR RAGE IN THE CAGE! Fat Tony: WHOOOOOO. Eh? Jimmy: LISTEN TO THIS CALGARY CROWD! THEY ARE GOING NUTS! AND WHO CAN BLAME THEM? Fat Tony: THIS COMES BUT ONCE A YEAR, AND THERE IS TRUELY NOTHING LIKE IT. Jimmy: This is our superbowl, and there is no expense spared, no match too brutal, and no holds barred. Nearly 5 Hours of INTENSE WWA ACTION tonight, LIVE From this Saddledome. Fat Tony: Not to mention the match which was pre-recorded! Jimmy: THats right, CELL ina frozen HELL! Fat Tony: Holy mama - wait till you see that..... Jimmy: Lets just take a glimpse of the intense matches we have here tonight.... We see a graphic of The Cell facing off against Extreme Corporation Jimmy: First up, The Extreme Corporation take on there foes and rivals, The Cell. The Cell have had completely undeserved freedom on Fiesta, thanks to their allegiance with Antonio Cruz. And SOmers and Harley just had no TV Time - no bookings, and no appearances. After there sit-in last week, Enough was enough, and now they get to square off agaisnt each other, with no disqualifications. Next graphic pops up. Shaman, Nick Adams, Christyan Payne, and Grunge, with "TV TITLE" Spread across the front Jimmy: Next up, Christyan Payne will finally have to fight fairly for the TV Title, after weeks of cheating and manipulation.... Fat Tony: You mean Cunning and Genious right? Jimmy: CHEATING..... Wiith No mack Tenn to help him out, and with Shaman, Adams and Grunge allw anting a piece of Payne, I wouldn;t be surprised if we saw a title change hands here tonight.... The graphic changes to see a graphic of Chance, Jimmi Zion and Enigma. The TV and Cruiserweight titles are showing dominantly. Jimmy: 3 Men, Two Titles, One match. These three have been feuding over these titles for weeks now, and this match will have ONE WINNER walking away with TWO belts. But who will it be? All three are masters of in ring acrobatics, and none of them are frightened to risk it all to win those belts. So who will it be? Fat Tony: ENIGMA BABY! YEAH! A graphic of 6 men. Phobos, Deimos, Mighty Joe, Venny LeTrav, and Two black silhouettes Fat Tony: havoc have showed there domination of the tag ranks for months, the only thing they have been lacking is the gold. With The Luchadores not competeing in-ring for a while, will they have it in them to win the match? Can they beat havoc? And who are the mystery team? Jimmy: Rumors have been flying round the internet for.... Fat Tony: Meh - who needs rumors when everyone knows Havoc is gonna win? The next picture shows Shane Dorian and Tracer Bullet Jimmy: Tracer Bullet will finally get his chance to have a go at Dorian one on one. Dorian has been terrorising tracer recently, and Tracer is becoming visibly upset by it, and at long last he can make Dorian tap out. Fat Tony: HAHAHAHAHA Jimmy: What's so funny? Fat Tony: Dorian? Tap? You gotta be kidding me! Next we see Damian Thorne and Kash, with the US Title displayed Fat Tony: Man oh man, I cannot WAIT to see this one.... Jimmy: Thorne hates kash. Kash hates Thorne. Facts. Thornes original Target was the US Title, but it has changed to so much more than that. A deep hatred for kash runs through Thornes blood, and the leader of thr House of Pain is surely the favourite for this match..... Fat Tony: Of course! Jimmy: But with that said, Kash has an EQUAL hatred. Can the Wiccan come through and beat the odds? Fat Tony: No WAY! Thorne is a psycho.... Next we see Denrol and Kraven Jimmy: Next up, the match we had the absolute horror of witnessing last night.... Fat Tony: Horror? it was GREAT! Jimmy: These two men, in an icy desertin a cell that is freezing, encased in barbed wire. You will not find a more brutal match than this. When you watch it, your eyes will cringe. Fat Tony: Either that or cheer at every disgusting moment...... Jimmy: These two men loathe each other, and tonight you'll see who comes out on top, once and for all. And Finally, Ian Tense and Wes Sadners Fat Tony: And speaking of hate, this main event doesn;t seem to have as much! Jimmy: These two men, both incredible athletes, have a mutual respect, but simultaneously, want to beat the crap out of each other. The sarcasm is intentional and meant to be offensive, and both men constantly try to out do each other, but in this match, who can out-do each other the most times? Fat Tony: Uh - well constructed sentence there James... Jimmy: Shut up - Wes Sanders, Ian Tense, WWA Title. TONIGHT! We suddenly cut. Grunge is backstage leaning back in a steel chair with his head-phones on listining to music. He has his eyes closed and is humming along. Wallace Coleman walks up. Coleman: Grunge a word. Grunge doesn't hear him. He continues to listen humming along. Wallace taps on Grunge's shoulder to try and get his attention. Coleman: Grunge...whoah. Grunge grabs his arm and stands up with the speed of a snake. He has a deadly look up his face, standing nose to nose with Coleman. Grunge lets his arm go and takes off his head phones. Coleman: ...Umm..just a word. Grunge stands dead serious for a moment before he flashes a smile. Julie: Gum? Grunge holds up a package of Trident. Wallace shakes his head. Coleman: Uh no. Grunge just shrugs and pops one is his mouth smiling and smacking loudly. Coleman: Well tonight you have a match for the TV title and.... Julie: Bingo. Coleman: Well yes I was just wondering how you feel about tonight and...... At this Julie walks aup grabs her husband by the shoulder. Grunge doesn't take his eyes off Coleman. Julie: Time to go. Grunge holds up his hand. Julie: I can hear the music fading out. My dreams fading in. Sure you don't want some gum? Wallace shakes his head as Grunge and Julie walk off, as we cut back to the announcers. Jimmy: Folks, we are about to get started! Fat Tony: I can hardly wait! Heavy Cell doors smash together on the WWAtron then explode as white pyros blast across the ramps. The words “BRUTALLY TRUTHFUL” appear on the screen. Metallica’s No Remorse can be heard. No mercy for what we are doing No thought to even what we have done We don't need to feel the sorrow No remorse for the helpless one Leviathan and Lockdown, the vicious members of the “CELL” step out from behind the curtains. They look at each other and laugh as the crowd start booing. Almost drowning out the music. War without end No remorse No repent We don't care what it meant Another day Another death Another sorrow Another breath No remorse No repent We don't care what it meant Another day Another death Another sorrow Another breath The “CELL” head to the ring, Lockdown leads the behemoth, Leviathan, who casually follows. They curse and sneer as the audience abuses them as they walk down the ramp. A sign is shown, “WARR LOCK THE CELL UP”. Blood feeds the war machine As it eats its way across the land We don't need the feel the sorrow No remorse is the one command Jimmy: These two have turned Fiesta into their own personal playground. Fat Tony: Personally, I love their work. It reminds me of someone. Jimmy: Charles Manson? Fat Tony: Good one, but remember Tony is color and Jimmy play by play. Jimmy: It’s Rage in the Cage; all rules are out the window. Fat Tony: And reality. The Cell enters the Cage. Lockdown checks the cage as Leviathan rests in a corner with both arms on the top rope awaiting the arrival of the “Extreme Corporation”. Fat Tony: My money is on these bad boys. Jimmy: Extreme Corporation is going to give them one hell of a fight. Suddenly all the lights in the arena go out and the stadium is eerily pitch black. The crowd roars and then quietens down in anticipation of what will happen next. The opening chords of "Damage Inc." by Metallica blast over the P.A. Dealing out the agony within Charging hard and no one's gonna give in Living on your knees, conformity Or dying on your feet for honesty Inbred our bodies work as one Bloody, but never cry submission! The pyro explodes in a stream of light and the words "EXTREME CORPORATION" form in blood on the screen and then trickle away. Following our instinct not a trend Go against the grain until the end Blood will follow blood Dying time is here Damage Incorporated!!! Gary Trudeau: And now making his way to the ring, one half of the Extreme Corporation, hailing from Long Island, New York. Weighing in at 250 pounds, he is the WWA Rookie Champion, Mal Sommmmmerrrrrsss!!!!!!!! From behind the curtain appears Mal Somers, who appears to be pumped up for his upcoming match, shaking his fists and pointing to the Cell members standing in the ring. Somers is dressed for battle, his shock of long wavy red hair messed up, dressed in his customary black boots and black tights and "Extreme Corporation" T-Shirt. The WWA Rookie Title is draped over his shoulder. The crowd cheers as Somers stands at the top of the ramp awaiting his partner Dave Harley. Jimmy: Where is Dave Harley? Fat Tony: Turned Chicken. Jimmy: I doubt that, Harley has heart. A laugh can be heard in the arena and it is coming from the WWAtron. On view is Commissioner Cruz. It appears he is looking directly down at Mal Somers. Mal instinctively turns around to see what is happening, as he stands all alone at the top of the ramp. Jimmy: What’s this? He is not wanted here. Fat Tony: He is the commissioner after all and a good one I might add. Mikal told me so. Jimmy: And Mikal is a good judge? Cruz begins shaking his head. Cruz: Jou think we would let your little protest on Meltdown go unchecked. Gringo, jou pi$$ed off the wrong people and El Prisedente Warr only made it worse. Mal Somers your retribution is nigh but first enjoy this. Outside the arena, a black limousine pulls into the garage. The driver makes sure the car stops directly at the door where two arena security guards are waiting, making sure only the right people get in tonight. Guard: This the one Louis? Louis: Yep, plates check. Hold on they are getting out. From the limousine steps Dave Harley and Mal Somers. Harley heads to the trunk to get his gear. Louis: Ok Eddy its Showtime. Eddy: Go for it. Louis: Hey, Are you Mal Somers? Somers: I am, why do you ask? Louis: Man, Warr just blew a trumpet up our behind. If we didn’t get you to him within five seconds of your arrival. We would be working the graveyard shift in a graveyard Eddy: Yeah, so you better hurry. Somers: Dave, take my gear to the locker room. I will go and see what the Warr wants. Harley: Sure Big shot! Somers hurries inside the doors. Harley is retrieving all of Somers’ gear from the trunk of the limousine when a familiar figure from Fiesta stands behind him. Rage is transfixed in his eyes. The “Extreme Beast” Leviathan wearing his ring attire. Leviathan: Mr Harley, YOUR IN TOO DEEP! Leviathan slams the trunk on Harley’s head and repeats the does five times. Harley goes limp. The driver comes around to see what the commotion is. He looks at Leviathan and feels the urge to try something. Leviathan turns and peers directly through the driver and with one finger point he retreats to the safety of the limousine. Leviathan: Unfortunately, Mr Harley, this is a message for partner, which regrettably will get to him too late. Now let’s have some fun. Leviathan pulls the lifeless Harley from the trunk and leads him backwards. Leviathan: Gentlemen, drum roll please. Eddy and Louis try a poor attempt at a drum roll. Leviathan lifts Harley up and performs the “Poseidon Adventure” on him into the trunk. Eddy and Louis turn away as the crumbled mess of Harley is swallowed in the trunk. Leviathan now walks around the driver and hands him fifty dollars. Leviathan: Here you go sport, go catch a movie. Jimmy: What has Leviathan done? Fat Tony: Man, that was beautiful. Jimmy: Somers is in trouble if he goes through with this match. Fat Tony: Fire up the EMT’s. Mal Somers is standing frozen and looks down as if he has no chance. The Cell joins Commissioner Cruz in laughing at him. They start daring Somers to enter the ring. Jimmy: Somers can’t walk in there, it’s suicide. Fat Tony: I wouldn’t. Jimmy: I second that. What is Mal Somers thinking? Fat Tony: Where to run. The crowd has a low murmur that slowly builds into a chant. The arena is building with excitement. Crowd: X-CORP, X-CORP, X-CORP. Jimmy: The crowd thinks Mal Somers can take them. Fat Tony: What would they know their Canadians. Somers turns to face the crowd and raises both arms in the air to form an X. The crowd roars almost blowing the roof off. Mal Somers charges down the ramp. Jimmy: Somers wants revenge. Fat Tony: The Cell wants blood. The referee is in the ring trying to hold back the Cell members as they taunt Mal from within the cage as he makes his way to the ring, and hesitates, hoping for the arrival of his tag partner any minute. The crowd still cheering “X CORP! X CORP! X CORP!” at the top of their lungs. Mal stands listening to the crowd for a second before reaching under the ring and pulling out a steel chair and slinging it over his shoulder. The crowd pops at Somers’ display of courage. Lockdown makes his way to the unlocked door, which Somers grabs first, slamming it into Lockdowns cranium, sending him to the mat. Jimmy: You have to admire Mal Somers here. Heading into the Lions den without fear. Fat Tony: I will be admiring him when he is looking at the arena lights on a stretcher. Jimmy: What’s Leviathan doing? Leviathan grabs the referee and whips him into the turnbuckle. Jimmy: Leviathan just DEPTH CHARGE the only official. Fat Tony: That’s no disqualification and now no interference. Jimmy: He is an official! Fat Tony: Well he is officially out cold. Leviathan picks up the referee and tosses him out the door, then taunts Mal Somers some more to enter the cage. Leviathan steps back to the centre of the ring as Lockdown rises. The referee is out cold. Jimmy: If Mal Somers enters it just plain suicidal. Somers hits the ring apron and approaches the door to the cage again. Lockdown who has regain his bearings charges at the door trying to catch Somers off guard. Mal Somers jumps off the apron as Lockdown tries to grab him. He grabs the door and once more slams it flush into Lockdown’s forehead, dropping him like he had been shot. Jimmy: Somers is on fire! Fat Tony: He has not entered the cage yet. Jimmy: At least he is trying. Lockdown is lying face first on the bottom turnbuckle. Somers takes the opportunity to continue slamming the cage door on Lockdown’s forehead. Leviathan drags Lockdown into the center of the ring. Jimmy: Finally Leviathan has directed his attentions at an opponent. Fat Tony: That is not a good thing, ask James Curzon. Jimmy: Very true. As Leviathan checks on his partner, Somers again reaches under the ring and also pulls out a table and a ladder, both of which he slides through the cage door. Leviathan stands and motions for Somers to enter the cage. Somers climbs the stairs and cautiously steps foot into the cage. Mal Somers tells Leviathan to move back, he obliges. Jimmy: People you can feel the tension in the ring. The crowd burst with approval as Mal Somers holds his chair with one arm above his head. Leviathan is not phased. Jimmy: Somers has found an old friend. Fat Tony: That’s illegal. Jimmy: Need I remind you it’s a no disqualification match? Leviathan stands poised on the other side of the cage as Mal Somers enters the door. He is begging for Leviathan to charge. It appears to be a stand off as both men refuse to budge. Lockdown is stirring on the canvass. As the bell rings, Leviathan charges Mal who, chair in hand, greets him with two thunderous chair shots to the head. WHACKT! WHACKT! Jimmy: Those shots would have stopped an elephant. Fat Tony: There was a lot of hate in them. Jimmy: These two have got plenty of that for each other. Leviathan staggers back to the ropes and Mal takes a moment to soak in the crowd. As he turns back around Lockdown attacks him with a clothesline. Mal Somers is knocked into the ropes from the collision. Fat Tony: Glory hog all you want, but always remember there is two brutes in there. Jimmy: Lockdown looks shaken up and is having trouble focusing. Fat Tony: Not surprising with blood in his eyes. WHACKT Somers managed to keep his senses and rebounds off the ropes and with chair belts Lockdown over the head to keep the sides level. Jimmy: Lockdown is down again. Fat Tony: That is cruel. Jimmy: What about what he did to Shaman? Fat Tony: Artistic License. Leviathan uses the moment as Mal is distracted and charges. He nails Somers with a psychotic clothesline, almost snapping Mal’s neck. Mal drops the chair and hits the ground. He scrambles to his knees and Leviathan stands in front of him. Leviathan starts punching Somers forehead. His fists are pounding very hard. Jimmy: He could break rocks with those. Fat Tony: Or Skulls. Blood starts to trickle down Mal Somers face as a crimson mask begins to form. Leviathan doesn’t appear to want to stop. Jimmy: Somers entered the Lions den without fear but the numbers game had to happen. Fat Tony: I hope they eat him alive! Mal Somers is dazed but uses a last ditch effort to stop the brutality. He low blows Leviathan who drops to the mat, as does Mal Somers on his hands and knees. The crowd begins chanting. Crowd: SOM-ERS, SOM-ERS. Jimmy: Somers is not going down without a fight! Fat Tony: He is evil. How dare he attack the deep jewels. Jimmy: No DQ buddy and he wants to win. Somers reaches his chair and stands over Leviathan. Leviathan has managed to get to his hands and knees. WHACKT! Crowd: 1 WHACKT! Crowd: 2 WHACKT! Crowd: 3 Mal smashes the chair across Leviathan’s back. The chair begins to warp but Leviathan does not fold, refusing to collapse to the canvass. In fact he slowly rises. Somers doesn’t stop. WHACKT! Jimmy: What can keep this behemoth down? Fat Tony: Maybe a sledgehammer. Jimmy: Somers doesn’t care anymore. This is pure hate. WHACKT! Leviathan is dazed but has managed to get to his feet. Somers drops the chair to the mat in front of him and walks over to Leviathan. He is shaking his head trying to regain his senses. Lockdown has also stirred. Jimmy: Somers gives Leviathan a shot to the mid-section. Fat Tony: Not the DDT on the chair! Jimmy: Hit is Mal! Mal Somers DDT’s Leviathan on the chair. The impact causes a gash to Leviathan’s skull. Somers turns Leviathan over and instinctively goes for the cover. Fat Tony: I told you he was dumb. Jimmy: Against these two, instincts are all that keep you alive. Lockdown stands and sees his partner being pinned, plants a big boot to the back of Mal’s head. Jimmy: Lockdown is up and helping his partner. Fat Tony: Numbers baby, Numbers. Mal rolls off Leviathan and stands. He and Lockdown start trading punches. Lockdown appears to be staggering, he then kicks Somers right between the legs. Fat Tony: And the extra point is good. Jimmy: So it’s ok for him? Fat Tony: I call it like I see it. Jimmy: You’re so unbiased. Fat Tony: Thanks I know, it’s very hard to be like that. Somers drops to his knees. Lockdown walks past him, wiping the sweat from his forehead then flicking it on Mal Somers. He yells abuse at Mal, which can be heard by the ringside crew. Lockdown: SAY YOUR PRAYERS MAGGOT! Jimmy: What’s that mean? Fat Tony: Nothing good for Somers. Lockdown stands behind Mal Somers as if preparing for an execution. Then unloads with a lethal kick to the back of Mal’s head. Fat Tony: FIELD GOAL! Jimmy: Sick, that is just plain sick. Dq or no Dq. Lockdown looks at Leviathan and Somers then heads out the cage door. Jimmy: One can only think what he is up to. Fat Tony: Hopefully some weapon of destruction. Lockdown retrieves from under the ring a table and pushes it in. He also recovers a sledgehammer from the outside and slides it into the ring. Leviathan has recovered and is standing. He picks up Mal Somers and powerbombs him. Fat Tony: This is going to be good. Jimmy: Somers is in a ton of trouble. Fat Tony: That’s the way I like it. Lockdown helps Somers back up and whips him into the ropes, the force also making him connect with the cage. Somers staggers forth, clutching his back, into a tilt a whirl backbreaker by Lockdown! Leviathan is now up and the two take turns stomping on Somers. Leviathan whips Somers into the corner turnbuckles but Somers runs up the first second and third ropes, turning as he reaches the top and planchas onto the charging Leviathan! Jimmy: Was that a desperation move by Somers? Fat Tony: No running like a chicken is. Realising that he can’t take both Cell members out by himself, Mal starts to climb the cage, with Leviathan and Lockdown in hot pursuit. He turns around and sees Lockdown scaling the cage below him whilst Leviathan has set up a ladder parallel to the cage wall. Mal kicks Lockdown in the face, knocking him from the cage to the mat. The ladder wobbles slightly as Mal takes a look at it. Suddenly, he propels himself from the cage wall landing squarely on the top of the ladder. For a split second the ladder shakes violently before Mal launches himself backwards off the ladder performing an Asai Moonsault that hits Leviathan below. Crowd: “HOLY $HIT! HOLY $HIT!” The three men are down. Mal Somers stands, as does Lockdown. Lockdown removes from his prison overalls an object. He slips it on his fist as Mal Somers charges at him unaware. Fat Tony: I hope that’s been washed. Jimmy: I don’t want to know where it has been. Lockdown thumps Mal in the side of the head sending him crashing to the mat. Lockdown then tells Leviathan to get Somers up. Leviathan holds Somers so Lockdown can smash the sledgehammer into his face. Jimmy: Mal Somers can’t possibly take anymore. The Cell weathered the early storm of Somers and is now methodically destroying Mal Somers. Fat Tony: They have been telling people to listen. Leviathan lets go as Somers crashes to the mat. The Cell set up the table near a corner. Leviathan picks up Mal Somers and places him on the table. Lockdown takes to the ropes with sledgehammer in hand. Jimmy: Just end the match. This is beyond brutal. This is sadistic. Fat Tony: I think they want to end Somers permanently. Jimmy: I’m afraid you are right about that. Fat Tony: Hold on picks up his mobile phone. Jimmy: What are you doing? Fat Tony: Calling my Bookie, it must be my lucky day that’s twice you have agreed with me. Lockdown steadies himself on the middle rope. Leviathan hammers Somers across the head to ensure he stays on the table. Jimmy: If you are squeamish turn away. Fat Tony: Nah, watch the carnage. A surgeon at work. Lockdown leaps but Mal Somers some how smashes a forearm into Leviathan’s face. He then drags him over himself to take the blow. Lockdown cannot stop and ploughs the sledgehammer directly into Leviathan’s back. The table doesn’t break. Leviathan reels in pain, as Lockdown looks on confused. Fat Tony: Somers is one lucky Ba$tard. Jimmy: The fact he is still fighting is unbelievable. Mal Somers takes the opportunity as the Cell is disorganized. He clotheslines Lockdown, sending him to the canvass. Double noggin knocker by Somers. Who climbs the top turnbuckle. He looks behind him to see this move has had little effect on the Cell and are charging. He twists 180 degrees, and then jumps off the top rope, catching both men with a spinning tornado DDT to the mat! Jimmy: Mal is one pi$$Ed hombre! Fat Tony: Jimmy, Tony adds color. Somers stomps on Leviathan, the force is so hard Leviathan probably has Mal’s footprint on his back. Lockdown is up and spins Mal around. Lockdown has a problem as Mal is pimped full of adrenalin. Jimmy: Belly-to-Belly Suplex by Somers! Fat Tony: Get up Leviathan! Jimmy: Now a reverse DDT by Somers! Fat Tony: This not good, Leviathan get up. Lockdown is closed to passing out. Mal Somers picks up the sledgehammer and looks at Lockdown. Jimmy: Lockdown is about to taste some of his own medicine. Fat Tony: Get that weapon out of there. Jimmy: Stick it to him Mal! Fat Tony: You’re sick. Mal Somers rams Lockdown in the ribs, then brings the sledgehammer straight up into his forehead. Lockdown reels back to the table. Mal clocks him again with the sledgehammer. Lockdown lands on the table, Somers positions Lockdown then places the sledgehammer on top of Lockdown as he climbs the ropes. Jimmy: Somers-Assault! Fat Tony: This could be over. Mal hits his signature move but is feeling the effects of the match. He is slow to capitalize and make an escape. He starts crawling for the cage door. Jimmy: Lockdown is out. Leviathan smashes the chair across Mal’s back. The chair shatters from the impact stopping Mal Somers valiant attempt to escape. Jimmy: Leviathan was just in time. Leviathan picks up Somers and looks around to the crowd. He pumps his arm as a signal to the crowd. Jimmy: Leviathan is going for the “DRILL”. Fat Tony: Send him to China! Leviathan plants Somers with the Drill but does not cover him. Lockdown is still down. Leviathan stirs Lockdown, who takes a swing at him. The crowd gets excited thinking the Cell will fight each other. Fat Tony: Get it together guys! Leviathan manages to calm Lockdown when he realizes who stands in front of him. Leviathan tells Lockdown to go to the outside to grab something. Lockdown goes outside and picks up two chairs. Jimmy: The Cell can’t keep Somers done. Fat Tony: They need to finish this. Lockdown re-enters the cage. Leviathan stands up Somers. Lockdown tosses Leviathan a chair and they stand either side of Mal Somers. Jimmy: Con-chair-to! Fat Tony: Music to my ears! Somers is sent to dreamland as Leviathan and Lockdown raise their chairs in the air in triumph. The lights dim and the opening chords of ‘Damage Inc.’ by Metallica play. The crowd pops as Dave Harley steps out from behind the curtain and runs down to ringside to help Mal. Jimmy: It’s Dave Harley! Fat Tony: Lousy Driver. Jimmy: The tide is about to turn. He stops at the end of the ramp way and stares Mal in the face. He grins and clutches at his injured ribs as he starts to back away. Fat Tony: I told you he was chicken. The crowd starts to boo as Harley shakes his head at Mal and walks back up the ramp disappearing into the back. Mal looks pi$$ed as he tries to fight off Leviathan and Lockdown by himself. Thorne's hands are almost to the top of the cage before Kash decides enough is enough. To a chorus of cheers from the crowd, the Canadian wildman hops up onto the bottom rope and uses it as a springboard to leap up to Thorne and grab him around the waist before falling backwards. Kash's weight carries both men off the cage wall with a huge german-suplex-like maneuver, driving Thorne head-first into the mat. - Grimacing at the pain in his leg, Thorne stomps angrily on his Canadian foe before straddling him and punching him in the face over and over again. The referee can do nothing but watch. - Kash mouths something to Thorne which really upsets him, causing the challenger to lunge. The champion responds by ducking the clumsy clothesline attempt and running the ropes. On the rebound, Kash ducks a second clothesline attempt, jumps to the ropes, then springboards to the side of the cage. Kash now springboards off the cage, hitting a devastatingly modified version of his 180 degree elbow smash which takes Thorne down hard! The crowd roar for the move, but it has nevertheless taken a lot out of the US champion. - Thorne begins to ram Kash's head repeatedly into the exposed buckle, opening up the gash that once held his eyebrow piercing, turning it into a gaping fishes mouth, spewing out his life blood. The cameras close in splatter-movie style, leering in as Thorne begins to jab his fingers into the wound, working them around, making Kash scream in agony. - As we watch, Thorne finally lets go of his bite. Blood drips from the circle of teethmarks on Kash's forearm, and the US Champion clutches his wounded limb in pain. Thorne doesn't let up, grabbing the Canadian wildman and nailing him with a double-underhook DDT! - Thorne casts his eyes around, sees the announcers' desks and nods to himself.....and jumps, sailing head over heels through the air towards the British announcers' desk! Thorne smashes back first through it, shattering it in all directions and sending the two commentators fleeing in terror. The crowd go silent for and Kash stops climbing and just stares, aghast at what lengths Thorne will go to in order to beat him. - There Thorne lies, all eyes on him, even those of Kash, who is still at his perch on the side of the cage. The crowd begin to chant "Holy $hit" and this finally breaks him out of his reverie. We watch as Kash drops down the last few feet and the bell rings. Scenes from Denrol Vs Kraven. Kraven steps out into the cold air, and we see that he is dressed for war. His face is covered by death's-head makeup, and he wears black denim shorts, a black sleeveless "No More Tomorrows" t-shirt, black kneepads and huge black biker boots. Black tape encircles his wrists, and he grins evilly as he strides the short distance to the cage. - The clearing is deathly still as Denrol steps out of his trailer. A few of the faces cheer, but otherwise there is silence. Denrol is wearing combat fatigues, namely black and blue camoflauge pants, black combat boots, a black long sleeve shirt, black elbow and kneepads, a black ski cap, and a black Tactical Deployment Vest. - Denrol moves quickly, his rage still fueling him, and runs up the side of the cage. Kraven is taken off-guard as Denrol continues up the side of the cage, with the hand still on his throat, and leaps over Kraven's head. As Kraven tries to keep up with Denrol's incomparable speed, Denrol uses the momentum of his fall down the other side to pull Kraven over into an armdrag! - Denrol finishes it with a DDT, smashing Kraven's head into the steel mesh of the gantry. A shower of blood rains down through the mesh to the ground below. Barely able to focus, Denrol grasps the steel bars, trying to muster the strength to climb. Kraven rolls onto his back, his arm flopping limply over. - Relentlessly, one hand after another, they both climb. Between his lighter frame and the gloves that protect his hands, Denrol reaches the dizzying heights of the beam first. Kraven pulls himself onto the beam after Denrol, and both men stand glaring at each other. Their eyes lock as they advance. In the center of the beam, with the barbed wire-wrapped kendo stick overhead, they explode into a fury of punches. With each strike, blood falls to the ring below. Kraven gets the better of the exchange and reaches out, grasping Denrol's neck once again. A stream of blood bubbles up and over Kraven's hand as he squeezes, his own blood mixing with Denrol's.Kraven grins malevolently and lifts Denrol into the air.Denrol reaches out frantically as Kraven roars. His fingers find the kendo stick, grasping desperately. He pulls the stick down, clasping the handle in both hands. Kraven inhales. The assemblage of superstars all hold their breaths, the good guys in terror, the bad guys in anticipation. Denrol smashes the kendo stick into Kraven's forehead, causing him to release his hold. Suspended by nothing, Denrol falls. Staggered by the attack, Kraven's foot slips, causing him to fall. Through space, their bodies plummet. The wind ruffles their hair and their clothes. A trail of sweat and blood falls alongside of them. The audience gasps simultaneously, shocked by what they are witnessing. - He pulls his upper body off of the mat with his powerful arms, looking over at the prone form of Denrol. He crawls to Denrol, and turns him over limply to his back. Heavily, he falls on top of the Canadian. The referee drops for the count. - Denrol strikes the cold, unforgiving ground outside of the ring, bouncing once before coming to rest on his back. Kraven slides from the ring, his chest heaving, and his eyes burning with some kind of sadistic bliss. He places a single foot on Denrol's chest. The referee finishes the 10 count, and kraven wins the match Now... I... know... why Now... I... know... why I... know... why... you... wanna... hate... me.
